Corporate Introduction
公司簡介
Kingdee International Software Group Company Limited (the “Company” or “Kingdee”) was established in 1993. It is listed on
the Main Board of the Hong Kong Stock Exchange (stock code: 00268.HK) and headquartered in Shenzhen, the PRC. Adhering to
the core values of “Acting in all Conscience, with Integrity and Righteousness”, the Company is committed to helping businesses
achieve their growth targets and let the sun shine on every company through dedicated services. It strives to provide them with
the most trustworthy enterprise service platform.

Through persistent efforts to explore China’s Cloud enterprise service market, Kingdee has retained the largest share in the
enterprise application software sector for fast-growing enterprises for 14 consecutive years, and has grasped the biggest share in
the enterprise-grade SaaS Cloud services industry for the second year. In addition, Kingdee’s diverse Cloud services and products
are the preferred choices of leading enterprises. They include “Kingdee Cloud Cosmic” (an epochal Cloud service platform for
large enterprises), “Kingdee Cloud Galaxy” (a digital innovative Cloud service platform for fast-growing enterprises), “Kingdee
Jingdou Cloud” (one-stop Cloud services platform for micro and small-sized enterprises), “Cloud-Hub” (intelligent Cloud office),
“Guanyi Cloud” (Cloud services for E-commerce operators) and “Cargeer” (Cloud services for auto dealers). With its strengths
in management software and Cloud services, Kingdee provides services and products to more than 6.8 million enterprises,
government agencies and other organizations around the world.

金蝶國際軟件集團有限公司（「本公司」或「金蝶」）始創於1993年，是香港聯交所主板上市公司（股票代碼：00268.
HK），總部位於中國深圳。以「致良知、走正道、行王道」為核心價值觀，以「全心全意為企業服務，讓陽光照進每
一個企業」為使命，致力成為「最值得託付的企業服務平台」。

在中國企業雲服務市場不斷探索，金蝶不僅連續14年穩居成長型企業應用軟件市場佔有率第一、更連續2年在企業級
SaaS雲服務市場佔有率排名第一。金蝶旗下的多款雲服務產品獲得標杆企業的青睞，包括「金蝶雲 · 蒼穹」
                                                  （劃時代
大企業雲服務平台）、「金蝶雲 · 星空」
                   （成長型企業數字化創新雲服務平台）、「金蝶精斗雲」
                                           （小微企業的一站式雲服
務）、「金蝶雲之家」（智能協同辦公雲服務）、「金蝶管易雲」（電商行業雲）及「車商悅」
                                         （汽車經銷行業雲）等。金蝶
通過管理軟件與雲服務，已為世界範圍內超過680萬家企業、政府等組織提供服務。

Outlook                                                                                          展望
The next decade will witness the boom of Industrial Internet. At the core of Industrial          未來十年將是產業互聯網的時代，我們認識到產業互
Internet is traditional business, rather than Internet; and the foundation of traditional        聯網的本質不在互聯網，而在傳統產業；傳統產業的
business rests on industrial value chain, not the enterprise; the key to industrial value        本質不在企業內部，而在行業價值鏈；行業價值鏈的
chain is connection and reconstruction, not ownership.                                           本質不在佔有，而在鏈接和重構。


Building upon the successful experience of linking 6.8 million customers, Kingdee                面向未來金蝶將基於鏈接680萬家客戶的成功經驗，
will work with enterprises to promote the reconstruction of value chain, shape the new           與企業共同推動價值鏈重構，塑造產業互聯網新優
advantages of Industrial Internet, and create a win-win situation with the traditional           勢，與傳統產業共生共贏，從而構建休戚相關的命運
industry. Thus, we build a community bounded by destiny and to help integrate the new            共同體，助力新時代新商業文明。
era of commercial civilization.

our team strives to realize the management’s aspiration for future growth, Kingdee will          鬥目標，也是金蝶勇往直前的不竭動力。

Management Discussion and Analysis
管理層討論與分析

(1) Key Financial Information                                                             一、 主要財務資料
     Despite the uncertainties of the world environment in 2018, Kingdee still made new     二零一八年面對世界環境的不確定性，金蝶仍
     breakthroughs in the transformation towards cloud services. During the reporting       在雲轉型上取得階段性新突破。報告期內，本
     period, the Group achieved 21.9% year-on-year revenue growth, with revenue             集團收入同比增長21.9%，其中管理軟件業務
     from management software business grown by 12.9% year-on-year, and revenue             增長12.9%；雲服務業務增長49.5%，金蝶雲業
     from Cloud services increased by 49.5% year-on-year. Kingdee further pursued its       務收入佔本集團整體收入的比重為30.2%，金
     strategic objective of deepening Cloud transformation as the contribution of this      蝶繼續向深化雲轉型的戰略目標邁進。
     segment to the Group’s total revenue reached 30.2%.

Board Diversity Policy                                                                       董事會多元化政策
The Company has adopted a board diversity policy ("Board Diversity Policy") since             本公司已於二零一三年八月採納董事會成員多元化
August 2013. The policy sets out the approach to achieve diversity in the Board which        （「董事會多元化政策」）政策。政策列載董事會為達
will include and make good use of the differences in skills, experience background            致成員多元化而採取的方針，其中將涵蓋且善用董事
and industry experience, gender, knowledge and other qualities of the members of the          會各成員所具備不同的技能、背景及行業經驗、性
Board. These differences will be considered in determining the optimum composition of         別、知識以及其他特質。在組成最理想的董事會時，

2.3	Response to Major Risks                                                                 2.3   重大風險應對
     During the year ended 31 December 2018, the Group audited and assessed the                   本集團在二零一八年根據企業風險管理框架，
     Company’s annual risk according to the enterprise’s risk management framework                對公司整體年度進行風險審核及評估，並制定
     and finalized measures for major risk response and control to prevent or appease             重大風險應對、監控舉措，防止或緩解本公司
     the possibility of occurrence of any major risk to the Company.                              重大風險發生的可能。


     The Company has steadily developed its operational business in the past year.                本公司在過去一年經營業務得到了穩健發展，
     Due to the fierce market competition of the industry, the Company’s market                   由於所處行業市場競爭較為激烈，公司在重點
     competition in terms of key customers and cloud services may be intensified and              客戶與雲業務方面的市場競爭可能加劇，客戶
     customers may continue to change their preference and have a higher expectation              偏好不斷變化，對雲業務服務價值期望更高；
     of cloud services; marketing costs of the Company’s cloud service are high and               公司對雲業務市場推廣成本投入較高，新業務
     the launch of new business is faced with inherent market risks; at the same time,            推出存在固有的市場風險；同時隨著本公司品
     as the Company’s brand influence is enhanced, its intellectual property rights are           牌影響力增強，公司知識產權受到侵害行為增
     infringed more frequently and infringers conceal or destroy tort evidence so that it         多，侵權人採用隱匿、銷毀侵權證據等方式，
     becomes more difficult for the Company to safeguard its intellectual property rights         增加了公司知識產權維權難度，可能會給公司
     and a certain financial loss may be caused to the Company.                                   帶來一定的經濟損失。

The directors of the Company are responsible for the other information. The         貴公司董事須對其他資訊負責。其他資訊包括年報內的所有
other information comprises all of the information included in the annual           資訊，但不包括合併財務報表及我們的核數師報告。
report other than the consolidated financial statements and our auditor’s report
thereon.

Our opinion on the consolidated financial statements does not cover the other       我們對合併財務報表的意見並不涵蓋其他資訊，我們亦不對
information and we do not express any form of assurance conclusion thereon.         該等其他資訊發表任何形式的鑒證結論。


In connection with our audit of the consolidated financial statements, our          結合我們對合併財務報表的審計，我們的責任是閱讀其他資
responsibility is to read the other information and, in doing so, consider          訊，在此過程中，考慮其他資訊是否與合併財務報表或我們
whether the other information is materially inconsistent with the consolidated      在審計過程中所瞭解的情況存在重大抵觸或者似乎存在重大
financial statements or our knowledge obtained in the audit or otherwise            錯誤陳述的情況。
appears to be materially misstated.

If, based on the work we have performed, we conclude that there is a material       基於我們已執行的工作，如果我們認為其他資訊存在重大錯
misstatement of this other information, we are required to report that fact. We     誤陳述，我們需要報告該事實。在這方面，我們沒有任何
have nothing to report in this regard.                                              報告。

to enable the preparation of consolidated financial statements that are free from

1   General information                                                            1     一般資料
    Kingdee International Software Group Company Limited (the “Company”)                 金蝶國際軟件集團有限公司（「本公司」）於一九九九年
    was incorporated in the Cayman Islands in 1999 as an exempted company                在開曼群島註冊成立為獲豁免有限公司。其營業地點為
    with limited liability. The address of its place of business is Kingdee              中華人民共和國（「中國」）廣東省深圳市南山區高新技
    Software Park, 2 Keji 12th Road South, Hi-tech Industrial Park, Nanshan              術產業園區科技南十二路2號，金蝶軟件園。
    District, Shenzhen, Guangdong Province, the People’s Republic of China
    (the “PRC”).

    The Company is an investment holding company. The Company and                        本公司為一家投資控股公司。本公司及其附屬公司（統
    its subsidiaries (together the “Group”) are principally engaged in the               稱「本 集 團」）的 主 要 業 務 包 括 企 業 資 源 管 理 計 畫 業
    enterprise resource planning (“ERP”) business, including development                 務，其中包括：軟件的研發與銷售及硬件的銷售、安裝
    and sales of software, sales of hardware, provision of implementation                服務、軟件諮詢服務、維修服務、升級服務及其他支援
    services, software solution consulting services, maintenance services,               服務；以及企業雲服務，包括企業資源管理計畫業務雲
    upgrade services and other supporting services; and cloud services,                  服務、大數據雲服務、財務雲服務以及移動辦公雲服
    including enterprise cloud, big data cloud, finance cloud and mobile                 務。
    office cloud.

    The Company has its primary listing on the Stock Exchange of Hong Kong               本公司的股份於二零零一年二月十五日在香港聯合交易
    Limited (“Stock Exchange”) since 15 February 2001.                                   所有限公司上市。


    These financial statements are presented in Renminbi (“RMB”), unless                 除另有說明外，本財務報表均採用人民幣列示。
    otherwise stated.

2   Summary of significant accounting policies                                     2     重要會計政策摘要
    The significant accounting policies applied in the preparation of these              編製本合併財務報表採用的主要會計政策載於下文。除
    consolidated financial statements are set out below. These policies have             另有說明外，此等政策在所呈報的所有年度內貫徹應
    been consistently applied to all the years presented, unless otherwise               用。
    stated.

2.1 Basis of preparation                                                           2.1   編製基準
    The consolidated financial statements of the Group have been prepared in             本集團的合併財務報表是根據所有適用的國際財務報告
    accordance with all applicable International Financial Reporting Standards           準則（「國際財務準則」）編製。合併財務報表按照歷史
    (“IFRS”). The consolidated financial statements have been prepared under             成本法編製，並就可供出售金融資產的重估，及以公允
    the historical cost convention, as modified by the revaluation of financial          價值計量且其變動計入損益的金融資產及投資性房地產
    assets at fair value through profit or loss, available-for-sale financial            （按公允價值列賬）而作出修訂。
    assets and investment properties, which are carried at fair value.

    The preparation of financial statements in conformity with IFRS requires the         編製符合國際財務準則的財務報表需要使用若干關鍵會
    use of certain critical accounting estimates. It also requires management            計估計。這亦需要管理層在應用本集團的會計政策過程
    to exercise its judgment in the process of applying the Group’s accounting           中行使其判斷。涉及高度的判斷或高度複雜性的範疇，
    policies. The areas involving a higher degree of judgment or complexity, or          或涉及對合併財務報表作出重大假設和估算的範疇，在

     being recognised on the statement of financial position by lessees, as the           承租人而言，由於對經營租賃和融資租賃的劃分已經刪
     distinction between operating and finance leases is removed. Under the               除，國際財務報告準則第16號將會導致幾乎所有租賃均
     new standard, an asset (the right to use the leased item) and a financial            須在資產負債表內確認。根據新準則，主體須確認資產
     liability to pay rentals are recognised. The only exceptions are short-term          （租賃資產的使用權）和支付租金的負債。豁免僅適用
     and low-value leases.                                                                於短期和低價值的租賃。

     policies are set out in Note 2.13 and 2.16 below. In accordance with the              際財務報告準則第9號（第7.2.15段和第7.2.26段）的過渡
     transitional provisions in IFRS 9(7.2.15) and (7.2.26), comparative figures           要求，主體未重述比較數字。

      Contract assets are recognised when the the Group transfers control                 當本集團在客戶支付對價前將產品或服務的控制權轉讓
      of products or services to a customer before the customer pays                      給客戶，且尚未獲得收取對價的特定權利時，確認合同
      consideration and has not yet got unconditional right to consideration for          資產。
      its performance.

      Contract liabilities are recognised when the payment is made or the                 當客戶在本集團向客戶交付貨品或服務前支付了對價，
      payment is due (whichever is earlier), if customers pay consideration, or           或有權獲得無條件的對價，則合同負債在付款或付款到
      have a right to an amount of consideration that is unconditional, before            期（以較早者為准）時確認。
      the Group transfers products or services to the customer.

      As a result, other than certain reclassifications mentioned above, the              因此除卻上述提及的某些重新分類外，採用國際財務報
      adoption of IFRS 15 did not result in any material impact on the financial          告準則第15號並未對財務報表產生任何重大影響，由於
      statements as the timing of revenue recognition on sales of products and            產品及服務銷售的收入確認時點無改變。
      services is not changed.

2     Summary of significant accounting policies (continued)                           2     重要會計政策摘要（續）
2.3 Subsidiaries                                                                       2.3   子公司

2.3.1 Consolidation                                                                    2.3.1 合併賬目

      A subsidiary is an entity (including a structured entity) over which the               子公司為本集團所控制的實體（包括結構化實體）。當
      Group has control. The Group controls an entity when the Group is                      本集團從或有權從與該實體的參與中獲得變動收益並有
      exposed to, or has rights to, variable returns from its involvement with               能力對這些收益產生影響時，則其控制該子公司。自控
      the entity and has the ability to affect those returns through its power over          制權轉移至本集團之日起，子公司被合併。其自喪失實
      the entity. Subsidiaries are consolidated from the date on which control               際控制權之日起停止合併。
      is transferred to the Group. They are deconsolidated from the date that
      control ceases.

(a)   Business combinations                                                            (a)   業務合併
      The Group applies the acquisition method to account for business                       本集團利用購買法將業務合併入賬。購買一子公司所轉
      combinations. The consideration transferred for the acquisition of a                   讓的對價，為所轉讓資產、對被收購方的前所有人產生
      subsidiary is the fair values of the assets transferred, the liabilities               的負債，及本集團發行的股本權益的公允價值。所轉讓

2.4	Associates                                                                         2.4   聯營
      An associate is an entity over which the Group has significant influence               聯營指所有本集團對其有重大影響力而無控制權的主
      but not control, generally accompanying a shareholding of between 20%                  體，通常附帶有20% － 50%投票權的股權。聯營投資以
      and 50% of the voting rights. Investments in associates are accounted                  權益法入賬。根據權益法，投資初始以成本確認，而賬
      for using the equity method of accounting. Under the equity method, the                面值被增加或減少以確認投資者享有被投資者在收購日
      investment is initially recognised at cost, and the carrying amount is                 期後的損益份額。本集團於聯營的投資包括購買時已辨
      increased or decreased to recognise the investor’s share of the profit or              認的商譽。在購買聯營企業的投資時，購買成本輿本集
      loss of the investee after the date of acquisition. The Group’s investment             團享有的對聯營企業可辨認資產和負債的公允價值凈額
      in associates includes goodwill identified on acquisition. Upon the                    的差額確認為商譽。
      acquisition of the ownership interest in an associate, any difference
      between the cost of the associate and the Group’s share of the net fair
      value of the associate’s identifiable assets and liabilities is accounted for
      as goodwill.

      If the ownership interest in an associate is reduced but significant                   如聯營的權益持有被削減但仍保留重大影響力，只按比
      influence is retained, only a proportionate share of the amounts previously            例將之前在其他綜合收益中確認的數額重新分類至損益
      recognised in other comprehensive income is reclassified to profit or loss             （如適當）。
      where appropriate.

      The Group’s share of post-acquisition profit or loss is recognised in                  本集團應佔聯營購買後利潤或虧損於損益表內確認，而
      the consolidated income statement, and its share of post-acquisition                   應佔其購買後的其他綜合收益變動則於其他綜合收益內
      movements in other comprehensive income is recognised in other                         確認，並相應調整投資賬面值。如本集團應佔一家聯營
      comprehensive income with a corresponding adjustment to the carrying                   的虧損等於或超過其在該聯營的權益，包括任何其他無
      amount of the investment. When the Group’s share of losses in an                       抵押應收款，本集團不會確認進一步虧損，除非本集團
      associate equals or exceeds its interest in the associate, including any               對聯營已產生法律或推定債務或已代聯營作出付款。
      other unsecured receivables, the Group does not recognise further losses,

2.13.4 Impairment                                                                  2.13.4 減值

     From 1 January 2018, the Group assesses on a forward looking basis                 自二零一八年一月一日起，對於以攤余成本計量和以公
     the expected credit losses associated with its debt instruments carried at         允價值計量且其變動計入其他綜合收益的債務工具，本
     amortised cost and FVOCI. The impairment methodology applied depends               集團就其預期信用損失做出前瞻性評估。減值方法取決
     on whether there has been a significant increase in credit risk.                   於其信用風險是否顯著增加。


     For trade receivables and contract assets, the Group applies the simplified        對於應收賬款，本集團採用國際財務報告準則第9號允

     be recognised from initial recognition of the receivables, see Note 3.1(b)         的預期信用損失，詳見附註3.1(b)。
     for further details.

2.13.5	Accounting policies applied until 31 December 2017                               2.13.5 截至二零一七年十二月三十一日前採用的會計政策

      The Group has applied IFRS 9 retrospectively, but has elected not to                    本集團已追溯應用國際財務報告準則第9號，但選擇不
      restate comparative information. As a result, the comparative information               重述比較數據。因此，本集團將繼續按照之前的會計政
      provided continues to be accounted for in accordance with the Group’s                   策對提供的比較資料進行會計處理。
      previous accounting policy.

      Until 31 December 2017 the Group classifies its financial assets in the                 截至二零一七年十二月三十一日，本集團金融資產分類
      following categories:                                                                   如下：


      •     financial assets at fair value through profit or loss,                            •   以公允價值計量且其變動計入損益的金融資產；


      •     loans and receivables, and                                                        •   貸款和應收賬款，以及


      •     available-for-sale financial assets.                                              •   可供出售的金融資產。


      The classification determined is based on the purpose for which the                     該分類的確定取決於取得投資的目的。管理層在初始識
      investments were acquired. Management determined the classification of                  別階段確定其投資的分類，在資產類型為持有至到期的
      its investments at initial recognition and, in the case of assets classified            情況下，該指定則在每個報告期間結束時被重新評估。
      as held-to-maturity, re-evaluated this designation at the end of each
      reporting period.

(a)   Reclassification                                                                  (a)   重分類
      The Group could choose to reclassify a non-derivative trading financial                 如果金融資產在短期內不再以出售為目的而持有，則本
      asset out of the held for trading category if the financial asset was no                集團可選擇將非衍生金融資產交易從持有交易類別中重
      longer held for the purpose of selling it in the near term. Financial assets            分類。除貸款和應收賬款以外的金融資產，只有在極少
      other than loans and receivables were permitted to be reclassified out                  數情況下才被允許從持有交易類別中進行重分類，這種
      of the held for trading category only in rare circumstances arising from                情況是由一種不尋常的、且在短期內不大可能再次發生
      a single event that was unusual and highly unlikely to recur in the near                的事件引起的。此外，如果本集團有意向和能力持有這
      term. In addition, the Group could choose to reclassify financial assets                些可以預見未來或重分類期滿截止的金融資產，那麼則
      that would meet the definition of loans and receivables out of the held                 可選擇重分類這些金融資產從而滿足貸款與應收賬款的
      for trading or available-for-sale categories if the Group had the intention             定義的持有或可供出售的交易類型。
      and ability to hold these financial assets for the foreseeable future or until
      maturity at the date of reclassification.

      Reclassifications were made at fair value as of the reclassification date.              該重分類是在重分類之日以公允價值完成的。公允價值
      Fair value became the new cost or amortised cost as applicable, and no                  則轉變為適用的新成本或攤銷成本，且在重分類之日前
      reversals of fair value gains or losses recorded before reclassification                無公允價值收益逆轉或入賬的虧損。在重分類之日明確
      date were subsequently made. Effective interest rates for financial assets              了將金融資產重分類為貸款和應收賬款及持有至到期類
      reclassified to loans and receivables and held-to-maturity categories were              別的有效利率。現金流量預估的進一步增長使有效利率
      determined at the reclassification date. Further increases in estimates of              得以有效地調整。
      cash flows adjusted effective interest rates prospectively.

(c)   Impairment                                                                                 (c)   減值損失
      The Group assessed at the end of each reporting period whether there was                         本集團會於每個結算日評估是否存在客觀證據顯示某項
      objective evidence that a financial asset or group of financial assets was                       財務資產或某組財務資產出現減值。只有資產在初始確
      impaired. A financial asset or a group of financial assets was impaired                          認後發生了某項或多項事件（「虧損事件」）而出現減值
      and impairment losses were incurred only if there was objective evidence                         的客觀證據，且能夠可靠地估計虧損事件對金融資產或
      of impairment as a result of one or more events that occurred after the                          一組金融資產的估計未來現金流量構成影響時，方可確
      initial recognition of the asset (a ‘loss event’) and that loss event (or                        定金融資產或一組金融資產出現減值並產生減值虧損。
      events) had an impact on the estimated future cash flows of the financial                        對於分類為可供出售之權益投資，證券公允值顯著下跌
      asset or group of financial assets that could be reliably estimated. In the                      或長期低於成本亦是資產減值的證據。
      case of equity investments classified as available-for-sale, a significant
      or prolonged decline in the fair value of the security below its cost was
      considered an indicator that the assets are impaired.

      Assets carried at amortised cost                                                                 按攤銷成本列賬的金融資產
      For loans and receivables, the amount of the loss was measured as the                            對於借款和應收款項來說，損失金額按資產賬面金額和
      difference between the asset’s carrying amount and the present value of                          估計未來現金流量以金融資產初始實際利率折現的現值
      estimated future cash flows (excluding future credit losses that had not                         （不包括尚未發生的未來信用損失）之差計量。資產之
      been incurred) discounted at the financial asset’s original effective interest                   賬面值下降，損失額於損益中確認。倘貸款或持有至到
      rate. The carrying amount of the asset was reduced and the amount of                             期投資按浮動利率計息，計量任何減值虧損之折現率為
      the loss was recognised in profit or loss. If a loan or held-to-maturity                         合約下釐定之現行有效利率。實務中，本集團亦可以採
      investment had a variable interest rate, the discount rate for measuring                         用觀察到的市場價值確定某項金融工具的公允值，並以
      any impairment loss was the current effective interest rate determined                           此作為基準計算減值。
      under the contract. As a practical expedient, the Group could measure
      impairment on the basis of an instrument’s fair value using an observable
      market price.

      If, in a subsequent period, the amount of the impairment loss decreased                          如其後減值虧損降低，並證實與在確認減值虧損後出現
      and the decrease could be related objectively to an event occurring after                        的事件相關（例如債務人信貸評級改善），則過往確認
      the impairment was recognised (such as an improvement in the debtor’s                            之減值虧損於損益表內確認。
      credit rating), the reversal of the previously recognised impairment loss
      was recognised in profit or loss.

      Impairment testing of trade receivables is described in Note 3.1(b).                             關於應收賬款減值測試，詳情見附註3.1(b)

      Assets classified as available-for-sale                                                          歸為可供出售的資產
      If there was objective evidence of impairment for available-for-sale                             如有客觀證據顯示可供出售金融資產已減值，則累計虧
      financial assets, the cumulative loss – measured as the difference between                       損 － 即購入成本與當前公允價值減去該財金融資產先
      the acquisition cost and the current fair value, less any impairment loss                        前於損益表確認之減值虧損的差額 － 自權益轉撥至損
      on that financial asset previously recognised in profit or loss – was                            益。
      removed from equity and recognised in profit or loss.

      Impairment losses on equity instruments that were recognised in profit or                        減值損失計入損益表的權益工具不通過損益轉回。

     right to offset the recognised amounts and there is an intention to settle         負債可互相抵銷，並在財務狀況表報告其淨額。法定可
     on a net basis or realise the asset and settle the liability simultaneously.       執行權利必須不得依賴未來事件而定，而在一般業務過
     The legally enforceable right must not be contingent on future events and          程中以及倘公司或對手方一旦出現違約、無償債能力或

2.20 Borrowings                                                                         2.20 借款
      Borrowings are recognised initially at fair value, net of transaction costs           一般及特定借款按公允價值並扣除產生的交易費用為初
      incurred. Borrowings are subsequently stated at amortised cost; any                   始確認金額。借款其後按攤銷成本列賬；所得款（扣除
      differences between proceeds (net of transaction cost) and the redemption             交易成本）與贖回價值的任何差額利用實際利率法於借
      value is recognised in the income statement over the period of the                    款期間內在損益表確認。
      borrowings using the effective interest method.

      Fees paid on the establishment of loan facilities are recognised as                   設立貸款融資時支付的費用倘部份或全部融資將會很可
      transaction costs of the loan to the extent that it is probable that some             能提取，該費用確認為貸款的交易費用。在此情況下，
      or all of the facility will be drawn down. In this case, the fee is deferred          費用遞延至貸款提取為止。如沒有證據證明部份或全部
      until the draw-down occurs. To the extent there is no evidence that it is             融資將會很可能被提取，則該費用資本化作為流動資金
      probable that some or all of the facility will be drawn down, the fee is              服務的預付款，並按有關的融資期間攤銷。
      capitalised as a pre-payment for liquidity services and amortised over the
      period of the facility to which it relates.

      The fair value of the liability portion of a convertible bond is determined           可換股債券負債部份的公允價值按照等同之非可換股債
      using a market interest rate for an equivalent non-convertible bond.                  券的市場利率釐定。此金額按攤銷成本記錄為負債，直
      This amount is recorded as a liability on an amortised cost basis until               至債券被兌換或到期而終止。餘下所得款分攤至兌換
      extinguished on conversion or maturity of the bond. The remainder of the              權。此款項在股東權益中確認和記錄，並扣除所得稅影
      proceeds is allocated to the conversion option. This is recognised and                響。
      included in shareholders’ equity, net of income tax effects.

      Borrowings are removed from the consolidated statement of financial                   當合同列明的債務被解除、取消或到期時，借款自資產
      position when the obligation specified in the contract is discharged,                 負債表中刪除。已消除或轉撥至另一方的金融負債的賬
      cancelled or expired. The difference between the carrying amount of a                 面值與已付對價的差額，包括任何已轉撥的非現金資產
      financial liability that has been extinguished or transferred to another party        或承擔的負債，在損益中確認為其他收入或融資成本。
      and the consideration paid, including any non-cash assets transferred
      or liabilities assumed, is recognised in profit or loss as other income or
      finance costs.

      Where the terms of a financial liability are renegotiated and the entity              如金融負債的條款重新商討，而主體向債權人發行股權
      issues equity instruments to a creditor to extinguish all or part of the              票據，以消除全部或部份負債（權益與債務掉期），該
      liability (debt for equity swap), a gain or loss is recognised in profit or           項金融負債賬面值與所發行股權票據公允價值的差額在
      loss, which is measured as the difference between the carrying amount of              損益表中確認為利得或損失。
      the financial liability and the fair value of the equity instruments issued.

2.21 Borrowing costs                                                                  2.21 借款成本

    General and specific borrowing costs directly attributable to the                     直接歸屬於收購、興建或生產合資格資產（指必須經一
    acquisition, construction or production of qualifying assets, which are               段長時間處理以作其預定用途或銷售的資產）的借款成
    assets that necessarily take a substantial period of time to get ready for            本，加入該等資產的成本內，直至資產大致上備妥供其
    their intended use or sale, are added to the cost of those assets, until              預定用途或銷售為止。
    such time as the assets are substantially ready for their intended use or
    sale.

    borrowings pending their expenditure on qualifying assets is deducted                 的投資收入，應自合資格資本化的借款成本中扣除。
    from the borrowing costs eligible for capitalisation.

    All other borrowing costs are recognised in profit or loss in the period in           所有其他借款成本在產生期內的損益中確認。
    which they are incurred.

2.23 Current and deferred income tax                                                     2.23 當期及遞延所得稅

      The tax expense for the period comprises current and deferred tax. Tax is                本期間的稅項支出包括當期和遞延稅項。稅項在損益表
      recognised in the consolidated income statement, except to the extent that               中確認，但與在其他綜合收益中或直接在權益中確認的
      it relates to items recognised in other comprehensive income or directly                 項目有關者則除外。在該情況下，稅項亦分別在其他綜
      in equity. In this case, the tax is also recognised in other comprehensive               合收益或直接在權益中確認。
      income or directly in equity, respectively.

(a) Current income tax                                                                   (a)   當期所得稅
      The current income tax charge is calculated on the basis of the tax laws                 當期所得稅支出根據本公司的子公司及聯營經營及產生
      enacted or substantively enacted at the balance sheet date in the countries              應課稅收入的國家於資產負債表日已頒佈或實質上已頒
      where the Company and its subsidiaries operate and generate taxable                      佈的稅務法例計算。管理層就適用稅務法例解釋所規限
      income. Management periodically evaluates positions taken in tax returns                 的情況定期評估報稅表的狀況，並在適用情況下根據預
      with respect to situations in which applicable tax regulation is subject to              期須向稅務機關支付的稅款設定準備。
      interpretation. It establishes provisions where appropriate on the basis of
      amounts expected to be paid to the tax authorities.

      liabilities and their carrying amounts in the consolidated financial                     差異。然而，若遞延所得稅來自在交易（不包括業務合
      statements. However, deferred tax liabilities are not recognised if they                 併）中對資產或負債的初始確認，而在交易時不影響會
      arise from the initial recognition of goodwill, the deferred income tax is               計損益或應課稅利潤或損失，則不作記賬。遞延所得稅
      not accounted for if it arises from initial recognition of an asset or liability         採用在資產負債表日前已頒佈或實質上已頒佈，並在有
      in a transaction other than a business combination that at the time of                   關的遞延所得稅資產實現或遞延所得稅負債結算時預期
      the transaction affects neither accounting nor taxable profit or loss.                   將會適用的稅率（及法例）而釐定。
      Deferred income tax is determined using tax rates (and laws) that have

2.26	Provisions                                                                        2.26 準備
      Provisions are recognised when: the Group has a present legal or                       當本集團因已發生的事件而產生現有的法律或推定義
      constructive obligation as a result of past events; it is probable that an             務；很可能需要有資源的流出以結算義務；及金額已被
      outflow of resources will be required to settle the obligation; and the                可靠估計時，當作出準備。準備不會就未來經營虧損確
      amount has been reliably estimated. Provisions are not recognised for                  認準備。
      future operating losses.

      Where there are a number of similar obligations, the likelihood that an                如有多項類似義務，其需要在結算中有資源流出的可能
      outflow will be required in settlement is determined by considering the                性，則可根據義務的類別整體考慮。即使在同一義務類
      class of obligations as a whole. A provision is recognised even if the                 別所包含的任何一個項目相關的資源流出的可能性極
      likelihood of an outflow with respect to any one item included in the same             低，仍須確認準備。
      class of obligations may be small.

      Provisions are measured at the present value of the expenditures expected              準備採用稅前利率按照預期需結算有關義務的支出現值
      to be required to settle the obligation using a pre-tax rate that reflects             計量，該利率反映當時市場對金錢時間值和有關義務固
      current market assessments of the time value of money and the risks                    有風險的評估。隨著時間過去而增加的準備確認為利息
      specific to the obligation. The increase in the provision due to passage of            費用。
      time is recognised as interest expense.

2.31 Government grants                                                                 2.31 政府補助

      Grants from the government are recognised at their fair value where there              當能夠合理地保證政府補貼將可收取，而本集團將會符
      is a reasonable assurance that the grants will be received and the Group               合所有附帶條件時，將政府提供的補助按其公允價值確
      will comply with all attached conditions.                                              認入賬。


      Government grants relating to costs are deferred and recognised in the                 與成本有關之政府補助遞延入賬，並按擬補償之成本配
      consolidated income statement over the period necessary to match them                  合其所需期間在合併損益表中確認。
      with the costs that they are intended to compensate.

      Government grants relating to property, plant and equipment are included               與購買不動產、工廠及設備有關之政府補貼列入非流動
      in non-current liabilities as deferred government grants and are credited              負債作為遞延政府補貼，並按有關資產之預計年期以直
      to the consolidated income statement on a straight-line basis over the                 線法在損益賬確認。
      expected lives of the related assets.

2.32	Dividend distribution                                                             2.32 股息分派
      Dividends distribution to the Company’s shareholders is recognised                     向本公司股東分派的股息在股息獲本公司股東或董事會
      as a liability in the Group’s and the Company’s financial statements                   批准的期間內於本集團及公司的財務報表內列為負債。
      in the period in which the dividends are approved by the Company’s
      shareholders or board of directors, where appropriate.

       Previous accounting policy for impairment of trade receivables                        之前關於應收賬款減值的會計政策
       In the prior year, the impairment of trade receivables was assessed based             去年，本集團基於已發生損失模型對應收賬款進行減值
       on the incurred loss model. Individual receivables which were known to                評估。已知無法收回的應收款通過直接減少賬面價值予
       be uncollectible were written off by reducing the carrying amount directly.           以核銷。其他應收款進行統一評估，以確定是否存在
       The other receivables were assessed collectively to determine whether                 客觀證據表明已發生尚未識別的減值。對於這些應收賬
       there was objective evidence that an impairment had been incurred but                 款，其經評估後的減值損失計入單獨的減值撥備中。本
       not yet been identified. For these receivables the estimated impairment               集團認為出現以下任一跡象即表明存在減值證據：
       losses were recognised in a separate provision for impairment. The group
       considered that there was evidence of impairment if any of the following
       indicators were present:

       •     significant financial difficulties of the debtor                                •   債務方面臨重大財務困難；


       •     probability that the debtor will enter bankruptcy or financial                  •   債務人很可能破產或進行財務重組；以及
             reorganisation, and

       •     default or late payments (more than 90 days overdue).                           •   違約或逾期付款（逾期超過90天）。


       Receivables for which an impairment provision was recognised were                     對於已確認減值撥備的應收賬款，當本集團預計無法收
       written off against the provision when there was no expectation of                    回更多的現金時，則核銷該項應收賬款。
       recovering additional cash.

3.2 Capital risk management                                                            3.2   資金風險管理

      The Group’s objectives when managing capital are to safeguard the                      本集團的資金管理政策，是保障集團能持續運營，以為
      Group’s ability to continue as a going concern in order to provide returns             股東提供回報和為其他權利持有人提供利益，同時維持
      for owners and benefits for other stakeholders and to maintain an optimal              最佳的資本結構以降低資金成本。
      capital structure to reduce the cost of capital.

      In order to maintain or adjust the capital structure, the Group may adjust             為了維持或調整資本結構，本集團可能會調整支付予股
      the amount of dividends paid to owners, return capital to shareholders,                東的股利數額、向股東退還資本、發行新股或出售資產
      issue new shares or sell assets to reduce debt.                                        以減低債務。


      Consistent with others in the industry, the Group monitors capital on the              與業內其他公司一樣，本集團利用資本負債比率監察其
      basis of the gearing ratio. This ratio is calculated as net debt divided by            資本。此比率按照債務淨額除以總資本計算。債務淨額
      total capital. Net debt is calculated as total borrowings (including “current          為總借款（包括合併財務狀況表所列的「流動及非流動
      and non-current borrowings” as shown in the consolidated statement                     借款」）減去現金和現金等價物。總資本為「權益」
                                                                                                                   （如
      of financial position) less cash and cash equivalents. Total capital is                合併財務狀況表所列）加債務淨額。
      calculated as “equity” as shown in the consolidated statement of financial
      position plus net debt.

      The gearing ratios at 31 December 2018 and 2017 were as follows:                       在二零一八年及二零一七年十二月三十一日，本集團的

      The fair value of financial instruments that are not traded in an active                沒有在活躍市場買賣的金融工具（例如場外衍生工具）

      valuation techniques. These valuation techniques maximize the use of                    察市場資料（如有），盡量少依賴主體的特定估計。如
      observable market data where it is available and rely as little as possible             計算一金融工具的公允價值所需的所有重大輸入為可觀

      an instrument are observable, the instrument is included in level 2.

      data, the instrument is included in level 3.                                            金融工具列入第3層。

      －      Quoted market prices or dealer quotes for similar instruments.                   －     同類型工具的市場報價或交易商報價。

(e) Income taxes                                                                   (e)   所得稅

    The Group is subject to income taxes in several jurisdictions. Significant           本集團需要在多個司法權區繳納所得稅。在釐定全球所
    judgment is required in determining the provision for income taxes.                  得稅準備時，需要作出重大判斷。有許多交易和計算所
    There are many transactions and calculations for which the ultimate tax              涉及的最終稅務釐定都是不確定的。本集團根據對是否
    determination is uncertain. The Group recognises liabilities for anticipated         需要繳付額外稅款的估計，就預期稅務審計項目確認負
    tax audit issues based on estimates of whether additional taxes will be              債。如此等事件的最終稅務後果與最初記錄的金額不
    due. Where the final tax outcome of these matters is different from the              同，此等差額將影響作出此等釐定期間的所得稅和遞延
    amounts that were initially recorded, such differences will impact the               所得稅資產和負債。
    current and deferred tax assets and liabilities in the period in which such
    determination is made.

    Certain subsidiaries of the Group were each entitled to a preferential               集團的部份子公司在滿足特定條件下可享受一定時間的
    enterprise income tax rate for a specified period subject to certain                 企業所得稅優惠稅率。根據歷史記錄，管理層認為子公
    conditions. Management generally applies the applicable preferential tax             司仍滿足優惠待遇的條件和資格，故採用恰當的優惠稅
    rate to calculate current income tax and deferred income tax (Note 32) on            率計算當期所得稅和遞延所得稅（批註32）。在得知未
    the assumption that the subsidiaries will continue to meet the conditions            能滿足條件和適用稅率發生變動的當年調整計稅結果。
    and qualify for the preferential treatment as evidenced by past records.
    The consequence of any failure to meet the conditions and any change
    in the applicable tax rate is adjusted in the year in which the information
    becomes known.

      Impairment of goodwill                                                              商譽減值

      Goodwill arises from the Group’s acquisition of Shanghai Guanyi                     商譽來自於本集團對上海管易雲計算軟件有限公司的收
      Cloudcomputing Software Co., Ltd. (“Guanyi”) and was determined at the              購，其在二零一六年二月二十九日收購日確認。該數值
      date of acquisition on 29 February 2016, being the difference between               為管易雲可辨認淨資產公允價值與支付對價的差額。對
      the purchase considerations and the fair value of net identifiable assets of        管易合併產生的商譽已經通過現金產出單元進行了減值
      Guanyi. Goodwill has been primarily allocated to the CGU of Guanyi for              測試。
      impairment testing.

      The recoverable amount of a CGU is determined based on value-in-                    現金產出單元的可回收金額根據使用價值計算。計算方
      use calculations. These calculations use pre-tax cash flow projections              式利用稅前現金流量預測，依據管理層批核的五年期財
      based on financial budgets approved by management covering a five-year              政預算。管理層採用的五年預算期限是根據管易的未來
      period. Management determined a projection period of five years based               發展趨勢以及行業經驗所預計。超過該五年期的現金流
      on expected development trend of Guanyi and industry experience. Cash               量採用以下所述的估計的永續增長率作出推算。該增長
      flows beyond the five-year period are extrapolated using the estimated              率不超過現金產出單元經營的企業管理雲服務業務的長
      terminal growth rate stated below. The growth rate does not exceed the              期平均增長率。所採用之貼現率尚未扣除稅項，且反應
      long-term average growth rate for the enterprise management cloud                   有關行業的具體風險。
      services business in which the CGU operates. The discount rate used is
      pre-tax and reflects specific risks relating to the relevant industry.

      The key assumptions used for value-in-use calculations for 2018 are as              二零一八使用價值計算使用的主要假設如下：
      follows:

9     Investment properties (continued)                                              9   投資性房地產（續）
      Valuation processes of the Group                                                   本集團的估值流程

      The Group’s finance department includes a team that reviews the                    本集團財務部設有一個小組，專責就財務報告目的對
      valuations performed by the independent valuers for financial reporting            獨立估值師的估值進行審查。此小組直接向首席財務
      purposes. This team reports directly to the chief financial officer (CFO).         官(CFO)彙報。為配合本集團的中期和年度報告日期，
      Discussions of valuation processes and results are held among the CFO,             CFO、估值小組與估值師最少每六個月開會一次，討論
      the valuation team and the valuers at least once every six months, in line         估值流程和相關結果。
      with the Group’s interim and annual reporting dates.

      At the end of each reporting period the finance department:                        在每個財政年度末，財務部將會：


      －    Verifies all major inputs to the independent valuation report;                －   核實獨立估值報告的所有重要資料來源；


      －    Assesses property valuations movements when compared to the                   －   評估物業估值與上年度估值報告比較下的變動；
           prior year valuation report;

      －    Holds discussions with the independent valuer.                                －   與獨立估值師進行討論；


      As part of this discussion, the team presents a report that explains the           集團經討論後會呈交報告，解釋公允價值變動的原因。
      reasons for the fair value movements.

      The Group considers the probability of default upon initial recognition of             本集團考慮在資產最初確認時發生違約的可能性，以及
      asset and whether there has been a significant increase in credit risk on              在整個報告期間信貸風險是否持續顯著增加。為了評估
      an ongoing basis throughout each reporting period. To assess whether                   信用風險是否顯著增加，本集團將資產在報告日期發生
      there is a significant increase in credit risk, the Group compares the risk            違約的風險與初始確認日期發生違約的風險進行比較。
      of a default occurring on the asset as at the reporting date with the risk of
      default as at the date of initial recognition.

      As at 31 December 2018, due to no loans was defaulted and borrowers                    截至二零一八年十二月三十一日，由於沒有違約貸款且
      are considered having strong capabilities to meet contractual cash flows,              借款人均被認為擁有足夠的能力來滿足合同現金流，給
      the loans advanced to third parties are considered to have low credit risk,            予第三方的貸款被認為信用風險較低，因此損失限制在
      and the loss allowance recognised during the year was therefore limited to             12個月的預期損失（一個資產的預期壽命小於12個月，
      12 months expected losses (where the expected lifetime of an asset is less             預期損失是衡量其預期壽命）。在貸款期限內，本集團
      than 12 months, expected losses are measured at its expected lifetime).                對其信用風險進行核算，適當地為預期的信貸損失做好
      Over the terms of the loans, the Group accounts for its credit risk by                 準備，按貸款本金1%的減值撥備。
      appropriately providing for expected credit losses on a timely basis, and
      a general provision of 1% of the principal was provided against the loan
      principals.

            companies. The Group has entered into several contractual arrangements                         實體成立日起與它們及其股東訂立若干協定，該等協定
            with them and their equity holders since their incorporation. These                            不可撤銷且使得集團：
            contractual arrangements are irrevocable and enable the Group to:

            －     exercise effective financial and operational control over these                          －   對運營實體可行使有效的財務及經營控制權；
                  operating companies;

            －     exercise more than one half of the equity holders voting rights of                       －   對運營實體可行使超過半數以上的表決權；
                  these operating companies;

            －     receive substantially all of the economic interest returns generated by                  －   對運營實體進行業務支援從而取得其運營產生的
                  these operating companies in consideration for the business support;                         全部經濟利益；


            －     obtain an irrevocable and exclusive right to purchase the entire equity                  －   對運營實體的股權具有不可撤銷的獨家購買權；
                  interest in these operating companies from the equity holders;

            －     obtain a pledge over the entire equity interest of these operating                       －   從其股東獲得運營實體的股權作為協定下應付集
                  companies from their equity holders as collateral for all the payments                       團款項的抵押。
                  due to the Group and as security for performance of obligations under
                  the contractual arrangements.


            As a result of such contractual arrangements, the Group has rights to                          根據這些協定的約定，本集團享有可變回報，且有能力
            variable returns from its involvement with them and has the ability to affect                  透過其對這些運營實體（「結構化主體」）的權力影響這
            those returns through its power over these operating companies (“VIEs”)                        些回報從而實現控制。因此，本集團合併財務報表將它
            and is considered to control them. Consequently, the Group regards them as                     們視為控制結構化主體。
            controlled structured entities in the consolidated financial statements.

            Nevertheless, the contractual arrangements may not be as effective as direct                   該種協議控股或不如本集團通過法定所有權直接控股這
            legal ownership in providing the Group with direct control over these VIEs                     些結構化主體有效，中國法律體系的不確定性可能影響
            and uncertainties presented by the PRC legal system could impede the                           本集團對結構化主體的經營成果、資產、負債的收益
            Group’s beneficiary rights of the results, assets and liabilities of these VIEs.               權。本集團認為該協議控股符合中國相關法律法規並具
            The Group believes that these contractual arrangements are in compliance                       有法律效力。
            with relevant PRC laws and regulations and are legally binding and
            enforceable.
